- **prompt-cache**: Anthropic's prompt cache keys on the **longest stable prefix** of the context. Anything that changes invalidates the cache from that point on, so the cost-saving rule is simple: **stable content first, volatile content last.** This matters here because the loops are cold-boot-heavy (Meteor rebuilds, multi-minute `/verify` and e2e cycles) and re-read large, mostly-unchanging context each turn. ([sentinel-web/community-manager](https://github.com/sentinel-web/community-manager/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/prompt-cache.md) · ★2)

([nestharus/agent-implementation-skill](https://github.com/nestharus/agent-implementation-skill/blob/HEAD/src/bootstrap/agents/proposal-expander.md) · ★3)
- **proposal-follow-up-tracker**: Use this agent when a sales rep, deal desk owner, or sales manager needs to know which proposals and quotes need attention right now, with a drafted follow-up action for each. Trigger for: proposal follow up, which proposals need attention, stale proposals, PandaDoc follow up. Examples: "which proposals need follow-up", "what's stuck in PandaDoc", "draft follow-ups for stale proposals", "any quotes that never turned into a proposal", "who hasn't opened their proposal yet" ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/sales-pack/agents/proposal-follow-up-tracker.md) · ★55)
- **proposal-generator**: 웹소설 기획안 생성 전문가. 장르+컨셉+플랫폼 입력으로 3개의 차별화된 라이트 설계안을 생성한다. '기획안 생성', '컨셉 제안', '소설 제안', '아이디어 3개', '방향 제안', '기획안 비교' 시 사용. ([MJbae/awesome-novel-studio](https://github.com/MJbae/awesome-novel-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/proposal-generator.md) · ★161 · 4 copies)
- **proposal-generator**: Generates three differentiated novel or story-book proposals from concept, genre, target reader, and publication-format constraints. ([Anubis44197/kit_hub](https://github.com/Anubis44197/kit_hub/blob/HEAD/agents/proposal-generator.md))
- **proposal-heatmaps-analytics-fellow** ([suphiozdedee/antigravity-skills](https://github.com/suphiozdedee/antigravity-skills/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/proposal-heatmaps-analytics-fellow.md))
- **proposal-impact-reviewer**: Read-only proposal impact reviewer, fenced to the nine composed read leaves and zero writes. Given a circulating proposal's prp_ id (or the pending-on-me question), reads the proposal and its change set back, draws the operator's own governance footprint through the me reads, reads back the specifically-affected roles, domains, and policies, and returns a synthesized impact picture — what would change, and where it intersects the operator's work — carrying the ids needed to act on it. Never records a response or performs any proposal write; never decides or recommends the operator's answer; n… ([Luscii/cli-glassfrog](https://github.com/Luscii/cli-glassfrog/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/proposal-impact-reviewer.md))
- **proposal-investigator**: Investigates a codebase for one proposed code change and returns verified facts with file:line anchors, for the proposal skill to write up. Read-only. Use before drafting a proposal. ([sfmig/rse-plugins](https://github.com/sfmig/rse-plugins/blob/HEAD/with-diagrams/agents/proposal-investigator.md) · ★1)
- **proposal-issue-creator**: Creates GitHub issues from approved proposals. Called by /proposals command when user approves. Updates claude-mem and cl-active-rules.json. ([prgazevedo/claude-code-workflows](https://github.com/prgazevedo/claude-code-workflows/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/proposal-issue-creator.md) · ★1)
- **proposal-judge**: Independent LLM-as-judge that scores a proposal draft against the job and the proposal-writing rules. Returns a 0-10 score plus actionable critique. Writes judge-vN.json. Never rewrites the draft. ([bilaltahseen/upwork-proposal-generator](https://github.com/bilaltahseen/upwork-proposal-generator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/proposal-judge.md))
- **proposal-orchestrator**: 提案書作成ワークフローのメイン調整エージェント。RFP受付から最終アセンブリまで全フェーズを統括する。 ([yoshidashingo/stegoro](https://github.com/yoshidashingo/stegoro/blob/HEAD/samples/proposal-writer/agents/orchestrator.md) · ★5)
- **proposal-prep-jp-agent**: Japanese company proposal preparation agent — generates a 1-page brief with cited public data. ([Kokai-Data/japan-business-data](https://github.com/Kokai-Data/japan-business-data/blob/HEAD/plugins/vertical-plugins/kokai-data/agents/proposal-prep-jp-agent.md) · ★2)
- **proposal-reducer**: Simplify proposals following "less is more" philosophy to minimize complexity ([Synthesys-Lab/agentize](https://github.com/Synthesys-Lab/agentize/blob/HEAD/.claude-plugin/agents/proposal-reducer.md) · ★55 · 3 copies)
